    <section class="section" id="who" aria-labelledby="whoTitle">
      <header class="section__head">
        <p class="section__label" data-reveal>01 — Who I am</p>
        <h2 class="section__title" id="whoTitle" data-reveal>A student of systems.</h2>
      </header>

      <div class="who">
        <aside class="who__aside" data-reveal>
          <div class="who__portrait" aria-hidden="true"></div>
          <div class="who__caption">
            <span>Pakistan</span>
            <span>ICS</span>
          </div>
        </aside>

        <div class="who__body">
          <p data-reveal>
            I'm an <em>Intermediate in Computer Science (ICS)</em> student based in Pakistan.
            My pull toward technology has always been about understanding — how something works
            beneath the surface, why a system behaves the way it does, and what becomes possible
            once you actually understand it.
          </p>
          <p data-reveal>
            Most of what I know came from independent exploration rather than a syllabus:
            reading documentation, breaking things, rebuilding them, and following curiosity
            into unfamiliar territory. <em>AI, programming, cybersecurity and physics</em> each
            offer a different angle on the same underlying question — how do complex systems
            actually work?
          </p>
          <p data-reveal>
            I'd rather build a small, imperfect project that teaches me something real than
            read about one that doesn't. That's the pattern that has stuck: learn by building,
            understand by experimenting, and keep going.
          </p>

          <ul class="who__tags" data-reveal>
            <li class="who__tag">Independent learner</li>
            <li class="who__tag">Self-directed</li>
            <li class="who__tag">Building in public</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

        <article class="interest">

          <p class="journey__tag">Study · Practice</p>
          <h3 class="journey__title">AI Engineering & Architecture</h3>
          <p class="journey__desc">
            Reading, experimenting and slowly building toward a working grasp
            of AI systems and software architecture — the long, unglamorous
            middle of the learning curve.
          </p>
          <a class="journey__link" href="https://github.com/lightibulb"
             target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
            github.com/lightibulb
            <svg viewBox="0 0 14 14" width="11" height="11" fill="none"
                 stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round"
                 stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
              <path d="M3 11 11 3M5 3h6v6" />
            </svg>
          </a>
        </article>

      </div>
    </section>

        <blockquote class="philosophy__quote" data-reveal>
          I don't want to simply use technology. I want to

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   2. BACKGROUND
   Orbs are plain radial gradients (no filter: blur) — the gradient already
   fades to transparent, and full-screen blur filters are the single most
   expensive thing on a phone GPU. Position is driven by --px / --py so
   parallax never fights any other transform.
   -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

.bg {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  pointer-events: none;
  contain: strict;
}

.bg__grid {
  position: absolute;
  inset: -2px;
  color: var(--grid-color);
  background-image:
    linear-gradient(to right, currentColor 1px, transparent 1px),
    linear-gradient(to bottom, currentColor 1px, transparent 1px);
  background-size: 64px 64px;
  opacity: var(--grid-opacity);
  -webkit-mask-image: radial-gradient(ellipse 80% 70% at 50% 20%, #000 8%, transparent 82%);
          mask-image: radial-gradient(ellipse 80% 70% at 50% 20%, #000 8%, transparent 82%);
  transition: color 0.7s var(--ease), opacity 0.7s var(--ease);
}

.bg__orb {
  position: absolute;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: radial-gradient(circle at center, currentColor 0%, transparent 66%);
  transform: translate3d(var(--px, 0px), var(--py, 0px), 0);
  will-change: transform;
  transition: color 0.7s var(--ease);
}

.bg__orb--1 { width: 70vmax; height: 70vmax; top: -30vmax; left: -22vmax; color: var(--orb-1); }
.bg__orb--2 { width: 58vmax; height: 58vmax; bottom: -26vmax; right: -18vmax; color: var(--orb-2); }
.bg__orb--3 { width: 46vmax; height: 46vmax; top: 42%; left: 40%; color: var(--orb-3); }

.bg__scan {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  opacity: var(--scan-opacity);
  background: repeating-linear-gradient(
    to bottom,
    var(--scan-color) 0px,
    var(--scan-color) 1px,
    transparent 1px,
    transparent 4px
  );
  transition: opacity 0.7s var(--ease);
}

.bg__vignette {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  background: radial-gradient(ellipse 90% 80% at 50% 30%, transparent 30%, var(--vignette) 100%);
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   4. NAVIGATION
   The blurred backdrop lives on ::before, NOT on .nav itself: any element
   with backdrop-filter becomes the containing block for position:fixed
   descendants, which used to collapse the mobile menu to 68px tall as soon
   as the page had scrolled.
   -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  top: 0;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;

          backdrop-filter: blur(18px) saturate(1.2);

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   5. SECTION SCAFFOLD + REVEAL SYSTEM
   [data-reveal] elements start hidden (only when JS is running and motion is
   allowed) and site.js adds .is-revealed as they enter the viewport. Once the
   transition ends site.js strips the attribute, so the element returns to
   plain CSS and its own :hover transforms are never blocked by leftover
   inline styles.
   -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

main { position: relative; z-index: 2; }

.section {
  max-width: var(--container);
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: clamp(4.5rem, 10vw, 8rem) var(--pad-x);
}

.section__head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: baseline;
  justify-content: space-between;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 1rem 2rem;
  padding-bottom: clamp(1.5rem, 3vw, 2.4rem);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rule);
  margin-bottom: clamp(2.4rem, 5vw, 3.4rem);
}

.section__label {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.6rem;
  font-family: var(--font-mono);
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.22em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--text-faint);
}

.section__label::before {
  content: "";
  display: inline-block;
  width: 22px;
  height: 1px;
  background: var(--accent);
  opacity: 0.75;
}

.section__title {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-weight: 400;
  font-size: clamp(2rem, 4.6vw, 3.2rem);
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: -0.015em;
  color: var(--text);
}

/* [data-reveal] reveals itself; children of [data-reveal-group] each reveal
   as they scroll in (grids, card rows). */
html.js:not(.no-motion) :is([data-reveal], [data-reveal-group] > *):not(.reveal-done) {
  transition:
    opacity 0.9s var(--ease-out-expo) var(--reveal-delay, 0s),
    transform 0.9s var(--ease-out-expo) var(--reveal-delay, 0s);
}

html.js:not(.no-motion) :is([data-reveal], [data-reveal-group] > *):not(.is-revealed):not(.reveal-done) {
  opacity: 0;
  transform: translate3d(0, 26px, 0);
  /* fix#6: prevent interaction with invisible, unrevealed elements */
  pointer-events: none;
}

html.no-js :is([data-reveal], [data-reveal-group] > *),
html.no-motion :is([data-reveal], [data-reveal-group] > *) {
  opacity: 1 !important;
  transform: none !important;
}

/* Filter / re-entry animation for cards that were hidden by a filter. */
.is-entering {
  animation: cardIn 0.6s var(--ease-out-expo) both;
  animation-delay: calc(var(--i, 0) * 35ms);
}

@keyframes cardIn {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: translate3d(0, 14px, 0); }
  to   { opacity: 1; transform: none; }
}

/* ==========================================================================
   Aman Sohail — Portfolio
   assets/css/themes.css
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH for design tokens and the three themes.
   Loaded by every page (index, about, skills, 404) before its own CSS.
   To retune a theme, edit it here — no other file defines colours.

   Themes
     dark       default · minimal charcoal and warm ivory
     rich       royal · imperial purple, polished gold, ruby
     cyberpunk  neon · void black, acid yellow, hot magenta, electric cyan
   ========================================================================== */

:root {
  /* Typography */
  --font-display: "Instrument Serif", "Iowan Old Style", Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif;
  --font-sans: "Inter", -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto,
               Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
  --font-mono: "JetBrains Mono", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, "SF Mono",
               Menlo, Consolas, "Liberation Mono", monospace;

  /* Motion */
  --ease: cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
  --ease-soft: cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
  --ease-out-expo: cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1);

  /* Layout */
  --pad-x: clamp(1.25rem, 4vw, 4.5rem);
  --container: 1180px;
  --content: 720px;
  --nav-h: 68px;

  /* Shape (themes override these) */
  --r-card: 12px;
  --r-btn: 10px;
  --r-pill: 999px;

  /* ── DARK (default) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────── */
  --bg: #0a0a0c;
  --bg-soft: #101013;
  --text: #f2efea;
  --text-dim: #96938f;
  --text-faint: #63615f;

  --border: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.09);
  --border-strong: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2);
  --rule: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);

  --accent: #e8e2d9;
  --accent-2: #c9a87c;
  --accent-3: #e8e2d9;
  --accent-soft: rgba(232, 226, 217, 0.14);
  --accent-contrast: #0a0a0c;

  --surface: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.022);
  --surface-hover: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);

  --grid-color: #ffffff;
  --grid-opacity: 0.024;
  --orb-1: rgba(232, 226, 217, 0.07);
  --orb-2: rgba(150, 160, 180, 0.05);
  --orb-3: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
  --scan-color: transparent;
  --scan-opacity: 0;
  --vignette: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55);

  --nav-bg: rgba(10, 10, 12, 0.72);

  /* Welcome-page surfaces */
  --card-bg: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.022);
  --card-bg-hover: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
  --card-border: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08);

  --btn-glow: 0 16px 40px -16px var(--accent);

}

   so the selected card never snaps back a few pixels when the animation ends. */

  45%      { scale: 1.028; }

html[data-theme="cyberpunk"] .btn--primary { box-shadow: 0 0 22px -6px rgba(252, 238, 10, 0.6); }

/* ==========================================================================
   Aman Sohail — Portfolio
   assets/js/projects.js
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Adds two enhancements on top of site.js:
     1. Count-up metric numbers
     2. SVG line-draw trigger for the EcoFac chart

   Both are progressive — the numbers render statically and the line renders
   fully drawn when JS is off or reduced motion is requested. No dependencies.
   ========================================================================== */

(function (window, document) {
  'use strict';

  var root = document.documentElement;
  var reduced = root.classList.contains('no-motion') ||
                window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- Countups */

  function formatNumber(value, decimals) {
    var factor = Math.pow(10, decimals);
    var rounded = Math.round(value * factor) / factor;
    var parts = rounded.toFixed(decimals).split('.');
    /* group thousands on the integer part only */
    parts[0] = parts[0].replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, ',');
    return parts.join('.');
  }

  function animateCountUp(el) {
    var target = parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-countup'));
    var decimals = parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-decimals') || '0', 10);
    if (isNaN(target)) { return; }

    if (reduced) {
      el.textContent = formatNumber(target, decimals);
      return;
    }

    var duration = 1200;
    var start = null;

    function step(timestamp) {
      if (start === null) { start = timestamp; }
      var progress = Math.min(1, (timestamp - start) / duration);
      /* ease-out cubic */
      var eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);
      var current = target * eased;
      el.textContent = formatNumber(current, decimals);
      if (progress < 1) {
        window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
      } else {
        el.textContent = formatNumber(target, decimals);
      }
    }

    window.requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }

  function initCountups() {
    var els = Array.prototype.slice.call(
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-countup]')
    );
    if (!els.length) { return; }

    if (reduced || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
      els.forEach(function (el) {
        var target = parseFloat(el.getAttribute('data-countup'));
        var decimals = parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-decimals') || '0', 10);
        if (!isNaN(target)) { el.textContent = formatNumber(target, decimals); }
      });
      return;
    }

    var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
      entries.forEach(function (entry) {
        if (!entry.isIntersecting) { return; }
        io.unobserve(entry.target);
        animateCountUp(entry.target);
      });
    }, { rootMargin: '0px 0px -15% 0px', threshold: 0.15 });

    els.forEach(function (el) { io.observe(el); });
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- Chart draw */

  function initChartDraw() {
    var charts = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.eco__chart'));
    if (!charts.length) { return; }

    if (reduced || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
      charts.forEach(function (chart) { chart.classList.add('is-revealed'); });
      return;
    }

    var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
      entries.forEach(function (entry) {
        if (!entry.isIntersecting) { return; }
        io.unobserve(entry.target);
        /* brief delay so the section reveal lands before the draw starts */
        window.setTimeout(function () {
          entry.target.classList.add('is-revealed');
        }, 200);
      });
    }, { rootMargin: '0px 0px -10% 0px', threshold: 0.1 });

    charts.forEach(function (chart) { io.observe(chart); });
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- Boot */

  function boot() {
    initCountups();
    initChartDraw();
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
  } else {
    boot();
  }
})(window, document);

FILE: assets/js/site.js
=======================================================================
/* ==========================================================================
   Aman Sohail — Portfolio
   assets/js/site.js
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Shared behaviour for inner pages (about, skills, 404). No dependencies.

     - theme switcher in the nav
     - mobile menu
     - nav background + scroll progress (one rAF-throttled handler)
     - scroll reveals via IntersectionObserver (compositor-driven CSS
       transitions; no GSAP, no leftover inline transforms)
     - orb parallax (CSS variables, eased in a single rAF loop)
   ========================================================================== */

(function (window, document) {
  'use strict';

  var root = document.documentElement;
  var reduced = root.classList.contains('no-motion');

  function $(sel, ctx) { return (ctx || document).querySelector(sel); }
  function $$(sel, ctx) { return Array.prototype.slice.call((ctx || document).querySelectorAll(sel)); }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- Theme */

  function initThemeSwitcher() {
    var Theme = window.PortfolioTheme;
    var buttons = $$('.nav__theme-btn');
    if (!Theme || !buttons.length) { return; }

    function sync(active) {
      buttons.forEach(function (btn) {
        btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',
          btn.getAttribute('data-theme-value') === active ? 'true' : 'false');
      });
    }

    buttons.forEach(function (btn) {
      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        var name = btn.getAttribute('data-theme-value');
        if (Theme.isSupported(name)) { Theme.set(name); }
      });
    });

    sync(Theme.current() || 'dark');

    document.addEventListener('themechange', function (event) {
      sync((event.detail && event.detail.theme) || 'dark');
    });
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------- Mobile menu */

  function initMobileMenu() {
    var nav = $('#nav');
    var toggle = $('#navToggle');
    var links = $('#navLinks');
    if (!nav || !toggle || !links) { return; }

    function setOpen(open) {
      nav.classList.toggle('is-open', open);
      document.body.classList.toggle('menu-open', open);
      toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
      toggle.setAttribute('aria-label', open ? 'Close menu' : 'Open menu');
    }

    toggle.addEventListener('click', function () {
      setOpen(!nav.classList.contains('is-open'));
    });

    $$('a', links).forEach(function (a) {
      a.addEventListener('click', function () { setOpen(false); });
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
      if (event.key === 'Escape' && nav.classList.contains('is-open')) {
        setOpen(false);
        toggle.focus();
      }
    });

    window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
      if (window.innerWidth > 780) { setOpen(false); }
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------- Nav + progress bar */

  function initScrollState() {
    var nav = $('#nav');
    var bar = $('#progressBar');
    if (!nav) { return; }

    var ticking = false;
    var scrolled = false;

    function update() {
      ticking = false;
      var y = window.pageYOffset || root.scrollTop || 0;
      var max = Math.max(1, root.scrollHeight - window.innerHeight);

      var next = y > 12;
      if (next !== scrolled) {
        scrolled = next;
        nav.classList.toggle('is-scrolled', next);
      }
      if (bar) {
        bar.style.transform = 'scaleX(' + Math.min(1, y / max).toFixed(4) + ')';
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener('scroll', function () {
      if (!ticking) {
        ticking = true;
        window.requestAnimationFrame(update);
      }
    }, { passive: true });

    window.addEventListener('resize', update, { passive: true });
    update();
  }

  /* --------------------------------------------------------------- Reveal */

  function finishReveal(el, delay) {
    window.setTimeout(function () {
      el.classList.add('reveal-done');
      el.style.removeProperty('--reveal-delay');
    }, (delay + 0.95) * 1000);
  }

  function initReveal() {
    var items = $$('[data-reveal], [data-reveal-group] > *');
    if (!items.length) { return; }

    if (reduced || !('IntersectionObserver' in window)) {
      items.forEach(function (el) { el.classList.add('is-revealed', 'reveal-done'); });
      return;
    }

    var io = new IntersectionObserver(function (entries) {
      var n = 0;
      entries.forEach(function (entry) {
        if (!entry.isIntersecting) { return; }
        var el = entry.target;
        io.unobserve(el);

        /* Stagger only items that arrive in the same frame. */
        var delay = Math.min(n * 0.07, 0.5);
        n += 1;

        el.style.setProperty('--reveal-delay', delay + 's');
        /* Force a style flush so the delay is in place before the class flips. */
        void el.offsetWidth;
        el.classList.add('is-revealed');
        finishReveal(el, delay);
      });
    }, { rootMargin: '0px 0px -10% 0px', threshold: 0.01 });

    items.forEach(function (el) { io.observe(el); });

    /* Exposed for filters: mark an element as already revealed. */
    window.__revealNow = function (el) {
      io.unobserve(el);
      el.classList.add('is-revealed', 'reveal-done');
    };
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------- Parallax */

  function initParallax() {
    if (reduced) { return; }
    var orbs = $$('[data-orb]');
    if (!orbs.length) { return; }
    if (!window.matchMedia('(min-width: 780px)').matches ||
        window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches) { return; }

    var depths = [22, 30, 14];
    var targetX = 0, targetY = 0, curX = 0, curY = 0;
    var running = false;

    function frame() {
      curX += (targetX - curX) * 0.06;
      curY += (targetY - curY) * 0.06;

      orbs.forEach(function (orb, i) {
        var d = depths[i] || 18;
        orb.style.setProperty('--px', (curX * d).toFixed(2) + 'px');
        orb.style.setProperty('--py', (curY * d).toFixed(2) + 'px');
      });

      if (Math.abs(targetX - curX) > 0.001 || Math.abs(targetY - curY) > 0.001) {
        window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      } else {
        running = false;
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener('pointermove', function (event) {
      targetX = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2;
      targetY = (event.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2;
      if (!running) {
        running = true;
        window.requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      }
    }, { passive: true });
  }

  /* ----------------------------------------------------------------- Boot */

  function boot() {
    initThemeSwitcher();
    initMobileMenu();
    initScrollState();
    initReveal();
    initParallax();
    root.classList.add('site-ready');
  }

  /* Never leave content hidden if something above throws. */
  function safeBoot() {
    try { boot(); }
    catch (error) {
      root.classList.add('no-motion');
      if (window.console) { window.console.error(error); }
    }
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', safeBoot);
  } else {
    safeBoot();
  }
})(window, document);

FILE: assets/js/skills.js

/* ==========================================================================
   Aman Sohail — Portfolio
   assets/js/skills.js
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Skills page: expandable cards, category filters, live counts.
   Loaded after site.js.
   ========================================================================== */

(function (window, document) {
  'use strict';

  var reduced = document.documentElement.classList.contains('no-motion');
  var cards = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('#skillsGrid .skill'));
  var filters = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('.filter'));

  if (!cards.length) { return; }

  /* ---- Expand / collapse ---- */
  cards.forEach(function (card) {
    var btn = card.querySelector('.skill__toggle');
    if (!btn) { return; }
    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var open = card.classList.toggle('is-open');
      btn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');
    });
  });

  /* ---- Counts come from the DOM so they can never drift ---- */
  filters.forEach(function (btn) {
    var key = btn.getAttribute('data-filter');
    var count = btn.querySelector('.filter__count');
    if (!count) { return; }
    count.textContent = key === 'all'
      ? cards.length
      : cards.filter(function (c) { return c.getAttribute('data-category') === key; }).length;
  });

  /* ---- Filtering ---- */
  function applyFilter(key) {
    var shown = 0;

    cards.forEach(function (card) {
      var match = key === 'all' || card.getAttribute('data-category') === key;

      card.classList.remove('is-open', 'is-entering');
      var t = card.querySelector('.skill__toggle');
      if (t) { t.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); }

      card.classList.toggle('is-filtered-out', !match);
      if (!match) { return; }

      /* Skip the scroll-reveal for cards the visitor is already looking at. */
      if (window.__revealNow) { window.__revealNow(card); }

      if (!reduced) {
        card.style.setProperty('--i', shown);
        /* Restart the animation even if the class was already present. */
        void card.offsetWidth;
        card.classList.add('is-entering');
      }
      shown += 1;
    });
  }

  filters.forEach(function (btn) {
    btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
      filters.forEach(function (b) {
        var active = b === btn;
        b.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
        b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', active ? 'true' : 'false');
      });
      applyFilter(btn.getAttribute('data-filter'));
    });
  });

  /* Clean up the entry animation so it doesn't fight :hover transforms. */
  document.addEventListener('animationend', function (event) {
    if (event.animationName === 'cardIn' && event.target.classList) {
      event.target.classList.remove('is-entering');
    }
  });
})(window, document);


=======================================================================
FILE: assets/js/theme.js
=======================================================================
/* ==========================================================================
   Aman Sohail — Portfolio
   assets/js/theme.js
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Shared theme system. Loaded in <head> on every page so the persisted
   theme is applied to <html data-theme="..."> before first paint.

   Also performs the two tiny <html> class updates every page needs before
   first paint, so pages don't need an inline script:
     - no-js  → js          (CSS can hide [data-reveal] only when JS runs)
     - adds    no-motion    when the visitor prefers reduced motion

   Public API (window.PortfolioTheme):
     .KEY                 storage key used for persistence
     .ATTRIBUTE           attribute applied to <html>
     .list                ['dark', 'rich', 'cyberpunk']
     .isSupported(name)   boolean
     .info(name)          { label, color, description } | null
     .label(name)         string
     .get()               persisted theme or null
     .set(name)           persist + apply, returns applied theme
     .apply(name)         apply only (no persistence)
     .clear()             remove preference and reset
     .current()           theme currently applied to <html>
   ========================================================================== */

(function (window, document) {
  'use strict';

  /* Pre-paint <html> flags. */
  (function () {
    var root = document.documentElement;
    /* fix#9: strict word-boundary regex; guarded class additions */
    root.className = root.className.replace(/(^|\s)no-js(?=\s|$)/, '$1js');
    if (!/(^|\s)js(?=\s|$)/.test(root.className)) {
      root.className = (root.className ? root.className + ' ' : '') + 'js';
    }
    try {
      if (window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches &&
          !/(^|\s)no-motion(?=\s|$)/.test(root.className)) {
        root.className = (root.className ? root.className + ' ' : '') + 'no-motion';
      }
    } catch (error) { /* matchMedia unavailable */ }
  })();

  var STORAGE_KEY = 'amanSohail:theme';
  var ATTRIBUTE = 'data-theme';

  var THEMES = {
    dark: {
      label: 'Dark',
      color: '#0a0a0c',
      description: 'Minimal, focused and professional.'
    },
    rich: {
      label: 'Rich',
      color: '#12072b',
      description: 'Royal purple and polished gold. Regal and authoritative.'
    },
    cyberpunk: {
      label: 'Cyberpunk',
      color: '#07040f',
      description: 'Neon-noir. Acid yellow, hot magenta and electric cyan.'
    }
  };

  var ORDER = ['dark', 'rich', 'cyberpunk'];

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function isSupported(value) {
    return typeof value === 'string' &&
           Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(THEMES, value);
  }

  function readPreference() {
    try {
      var stored = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      return isSupported(stored) ? stored : null;
    } catch (error) {
      return null; /* private mode / storage disabled */
    }
  }

  function writePreference(value) {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, value);
      return true;
    } catch (error) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  function removePreference() {
    try {
      window.localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    } catch (error) {
      /* ignore */
    }
  }

  function updateMetaColor(theme) {
    var meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="theme-color"]');
    if (meta && theme) {
      meta.setAttribute('content', THEMES[theme].color);
    }
  }

  function emit(theme) {
    var event;
    try {
      event = new CustomEvent('themechange', {
        detail: {
          theme: theme,
          label: theme ? THEMES[theme].label : null
        }
      });
    } catch (error) {
      /* CustomEvent constructor unavailable — degrade silently */
      return;
    }
    document.dispatchEvent(event);
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function apply(value) {
    var theme = isSupported(value) ? value : null;
    var root = document.documentElement;

    if (theme) {
      root.setAttribute(ATTRIBUTE, theme);
      root.classList.add('has-theme');
    } else {
      root.removeAttribute(ATTRIBUTE);
      root.classList.remove('has-theme');
    }

    updateMetaColor(theme);
    emit(theme);

    return theme;
  }

  function set(value) {
    var theme = apply(value);
    if (theme) {
      writePreference(theme);
    } else {
      removePreference();
    }
    return theme;
  }

  function current() {
    var applied = document.documentElement.getAttribute(ATTRIBUTE);
    return isSupported(applied) ? applied : null;
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  /* Apply the stored preference immediately — before the first paint. */
  var initial = readPreference();
  if (initial) {
    apply(initial);
  }

  window.PortfolioTheme = {
    KEY: STORAGE_KEY,
    ATTRIBUTE: ATTRIBUTE,
    list: ORDER.slice(),
    isSupported: isSupported,
    info: function (name) {
      return isSupported(name) ? THEMES[name] : null;
    },
    label: function (name) {
      return isSupported(name) ? THEMES[name].label : '';
    },
    get: readPreference,
    set: set,
    apply: apply,
    clear: function () {
      removePreference();
      return apply(null);
    },
    current: current
  };
})(window, document);


=======================================================================
FILE: assets/js/welcome.js
=======================================================================
/* ==========================================================================
   Aman Sohail — Portfolio
   assets/js/welcome.js
   --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Module 01 behaviour: loader sequence, entrance reveal, theme selection,
   confirmation dock, persistence hand-off and entry navigation.
   ========================================================================== */

(function (window, document) {
  'use strict';

  var Theme = window.PortfolioTheme;
  if (!Theme) {
    /* Theme system missing — do not trap the visitor behind the loader. */
    var stuck = document.getElementById('loader');
    if (stuck) { stuck.parentNode.removeChild(stuck); }
    return;
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* Environment                                                            */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  var reduceQuery = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
  var prefersReduced = reduceQuery.matches;

  var gsapLib = window.gsap;
  var motionOK = !prefersReduced &&
                 !!gsapLib &&
                 typeof gsapLib.timeline === 'function';

  var ENTRY_URL = 'about.html';   /* first inner page; point at home.html once it exists */

  var dom = {};
  var state = {
    selected: null,
    dockVisible: false,
    entered: false
  };

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* Helpers                                                                */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function $(selector, context) {
    return (context || document).querySelector(selector);
  }

  function $$(selector, context) {
    return Array.prototype.slice.call(
      (context || document).querySelectorAll(selector)
    );
  }

  function pad(value) {
    return (value < 10 ? '0' : '') + value;
  }

  function cardFor(name) {
    for (var i = 0; i < dom.cards.length; i += 1) {
      if (dom.cards[i].getAttribute('data-theme-value') === name) {
        return dom.cards[i];
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* DOM cache                                                              */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function cacheDom() {
    dom.loader = $('#loader');
    dom.loaderFill = $('.loader__fill');
    dom.loaderPct = $('.loader__pct span');
    dom.page = $('#page');
    dom.cardsWrap = $('#cards');
    dom.cards = $$('.card');
    dom.status = $('#status');
    dom.dock = $('#dock');
    dom.dockTitle = $('#dockTitle');
    dom.dockSub = $('#dockSub');
    dom.enterBtn = $('#enterBtn');
    dom.changeBtn = $('#changeBtn');
    dom.flash = $('#flash');
    dom.orbs = $$('.bg__orb');
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* 1. Loader                                                              */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function runLoader() {
    return new Promise(function (resolve) {
      var loader = dom.loader;

      if (!loader) { resolve(); return; }

      var settled = false;

      function finish() {
        if (settled) { return; }
        settled = true;

        loader.classList.add('is-done');
        loader.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        document.body.classList.remove('is-loading');

        /* Remove from the layout once the fade has completed. */
        window.setTimeout(function () {
          if (loader.parentNode) {
            loader.parentNode.removeChild(loader);
          }
        }, 700);

        resolve();
      }

      /* Failsafe: never hold the visitor hostage. */
      window.setTimeout(finish, 3000);

      /* The full loader plays once per browser session. */
      var seen = false;
      try {
        seen = window.sessionStorage.getItem('amanSohail:loaded') === '1';
        window.sessionStorage.setItem('amanSohail:loaded', '1');
      } catch (error) { /* storage unavailable */ }

      if (!motionOK || seen) {
        if (dom.loaderPct) { dom.loaderPct.textContent = '100'; }
        window.setTimeout(finish, seen ? 140 : 280);
        return;
      }

      var counter = { value: 0 };

      var timeline = gsapLib.timeline({ onComplete: finish });

      timeline
        .from('.loader__mark', {
          y: 14, opacity: 0, duration: 0.7, ease: 'power3.out'
        }, 0.05)
        .from('.loader__track', {
          opacity: 0, scaleX: 0.45, duration: 0.7, ease: 'power3.out'
        }, 0.12)
        .to(dom.loaderFill, {
          scaleX: 1, duration: 0.8, ease: 'power2.inOut'
        }, 0.15)
        .to(counter, {
          value: 100,
          duration: 0.8,
          ease: 'power2.inOut',
          onUpdate: function () {
            if (dom.loaderPct) {
              dom.loaderPct.textContent = pad(Math.round(counter.value));
            }
          }
        }, 0.15)
        .to('.loader__inner', {
          opacity: 0, y: -10, duration: 0.36, ease: 'power2.in'
        }, '+=0.05')
        .to(loader, {
          opacity: 0, duration: 0.5, ease: 'power2.inOut'
        }, '-=0.2');
    });
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* 2. Entrance reveal                                                     */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function reveal() {
    return new Promise(function (resolve) {
      if (!motionOK) {
        document.body.classList.remove('is-booting');
        resolve();
        return;
      }
      runReveal(resolve);
    });
  }

  function runReveal(done) {
    var timeline = gsapLib.timeline({
      defaults: { ease: 'power3.out' },
      onComplete: function () {
        document.body.classList.remove('is-booting');
        gsapLib.set('.topbar, .intro [data-anim], .card, .footnote', {
          clearProps: 'opacity,transform,willChange'
        });
        done();
      }
    });

    timeline
      .from('.topbar', { y: -14, opacity: 0, duration: 0.8 }, 0)
      .from('.intro [data-anim]', {
        y: 26, opacity: 0, duration: 0.9, stagger: 0.08
      }, 0.1)
      .from('.card', {
        y: 36, opacity: 0, duration: 0.85, stagger: 0.08
      }, 0.36)
      .from('.footnote', { opacity: 0, duration: 0.8 }, 0.78);
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* 3. Theme selection                                                     */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function selectTheme(name, options) {
    options = options || {};

    var meta = Theme.info(name);
    if (!meta) { return; }

    if (state.selected === name && !options.force) {
      showDock(name, options);
      return;
    }

    state.selected = name;

    dom.cards.forEach(function (card) {
      var active = card.getAttribute('data-theme-value') === name;
      card.classList.toggle('is-selected', active);
      card.setAttribute('aria-pressed', active ? 'true' : 'false');
    });

    if (dom.cardsWrap) {
      dom.cardsWrap.classList.add('has-selection');
    }

    /* Applies the theme, persists it and dispatches 'themechange'. */
    Theme.set(name);

    updateStatus(name, options.returning);

    if (!options.silent) {
      bloom(name);
    }

    showDock(name, { animate: !options.silent, returning: options.returning });
  }

  function bloom(name) {
    if (!motionOK) { return; }

    var card = cardFor(name);
    if (!card) { return; }

    if (dom.flash) {
      var rect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
      var fx = ((rect.left + rect.width / 2) / window.innerWidth) * 100;
      var fy = ((rect.top + rect.height / 2) / window.innerHeight) * 100;

      dom.flash.style.setProperty('--fx', fx.toFixed(2) + '%');
      dom.flash.style.setProperty('--fy', fy.toFixed(2) + '%');

      gsapLib.fromTo(dom.flash, { opacity: 0 }, {
        opacity: 0.26,
        duration: 0.32,
        ease: 'power2.out',
        onComplete: function () {
          gsapLib.to(dom.flash, {
            opacity: 0, duration: 0.9, ease: 'power2.in'
          });
        }
      });
    }

    /* CSS `scale` composes with the card's own transform, so there is no
       snap when the animation ends. */
    card.classList.remove('is-blooming');
    void card.offsetWidth;
    card.classList.add('is-blooming');
    window.setTimeout(function () { card.classList.remove('is-blooming'); }, 700);
  }

  function updateStatus(name, returning) {
    if (!dom.status) { return; }

    if (!name) {
      dom.status.textContent = '';
      dom.status.classList.remove('is-visible');
      return;
    }

    var label = Theme.label(name);

    dom.status.textContent = returning
      ? label + ' experience restored from your last visit'
      : 'Selected: ' + label;

    dom.status.classList.add('is-visible');
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* 4. Confirmation dock                                                   */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function showDock(name, options) {
    options = options || {};
    if (!dom.dock) { return; }

    var meta = Theme.info(name);

    dom.dock.hidden = false;
    document.body.classList.add('dock-open');
    state.dockVisible = true;

    if (dom.dockTitle) {
      dom.dockTitle.textContent = meta.label + ' experience selected';
    }
    if (dom.dockSub) {
      dom.dockSub.textContent = options.returning
        ? 'Restored from your last visit · change it anytime'
        : 'Saved to this device · change it anytime';
    }

    if (options.animate && motionOK) {
      gsapLib.fromTo(dom.dock, { y: 34, opacity: 0 }, {
        y: 0,
        opacity: 1,
        duration: 0.85,
        ease: 'power3.out',
        onComplete: function () {
          gsapLib.set(dom.dock, { clearProps: 'transform,opacity' });
        }
      });
    }
  }

  function hideDock(onDone) {
    if (!dom.dock || dom.dock.hidden) {
      if (typeof onDone === 'function') { onDone(); }
      return;
    }

    state.dockVisible = false;
    document.body.classList.remove('dock-open');

    function complete() {
      dom.dock.hidden = true;
      if (typeof onDone === 'function') { onDone(); }
    }

    if (!motionOK) {
      complete();
      return;
    }

    gsapLib.to(dom.dock, {
      y: 26,
      opacity: 0,
      duration: 0.4,
      ease: 'power2.in',
      onComplete: function () {
        gsapLib.set(dom.dock, { clearProps: 'transform,opacity' });
        complete();
      }
    });
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* 5. Actions                                                             */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function onChangeTheme() {
    hideDock(function () {
      // fix#3: reset selected state
      state.selected = null;

      dom.cards.forEach(function (card) {
        card.classList.remove('is-selected');
        card.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
      });

      if (dom.cardsWrap) {
        dom.cardsWrap.classList.remove('has-selection');
      }

      updateStatus(null);

      if (motionOK && dom.cardsWrap) {
        dom.cards.forEach(function (card, i) { card.style.setProperty('--i', i); });
        dom.cardsWrap.classList.remove('is-nudging');
        void dom.cardsWrap.offsetWidth;
        dom.cardsWrap.classList.add('is-nudging');
        window.setTimeout(function () {
          dom.cardsWrap.classList.remove('is-nudging');
        }, 1000);
      }

      var focused = null;
      for (var i = 0; i < dom.cards.length; i += 1) {
        if (dom.cards[i].classList.contains('is-selected')) {
          focused = dom.cards[i];
          break;
        }
      }
      if (!focused) { focused = dom.cards[0]; }
      if (focused && typeof focused.focus === 'function') {
        focused.focus({ preventScroll: true });
      }
    });
  }

  function onEnter() {
    if (state.entered) { return; }
    state.entered = true;

    if (!motionOK) {
      window.location.href = ENTRY_URL;
      return;
    }

    var timeline = gsapLib.timeline({
      onComplete: function () {
        window.location.href = ENTRY_URL;
      }
    });

    timeline
      .to(['.page', '.dock', '.footnote'], {
        opacity: 0, y: -16, duration: 0.55, ease: 'power2.inOut'
      }, 0)
      .to('.bg', {
        opacity: 0, duration: 0.7, ease: 'power2.inOut'
      }, 0.08);
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* 6. Pointer parallax                                                    */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function initParallax() {
    if (!motionOK || !dom.orbs.length) { return; }
    if (window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches) { return; }

    var setters = dom.orbs.map(function (orb, index) {
      return {
        x: gsapLib.quickTo(orb, 'x', { duration: 1.4, ease: 'power3.out' }),
        y: gsapLib.quickTo(orb, 'y', { duration: 1.4, ease: 'power3.out' }),
        depth: 16 + index * 12
      };
    });

    var frame = null;
    var pointerX = 0;
    var pointerY = 0;

    window.addEventListener('pointermove', function (event) {
      pointerX = (event.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2;
      pointerY = (event.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2;

      if (frame) { return; }

      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
        frame = null;
        for (var i = 0; i < setters.length; i += 1) {
          setters[i].x(pointerX * setters[i].depth);
          setters[i].y(pointerY * setters[i].depth);
        }
      });
    }, { passive: true });
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* 7. Events                                                              */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function bindEvents() {
    dom.cards.forEach(function (card) {
      card.addEventListener('click', function () {
        selectTheme(card.getAttribute('data-theme-value'), {});
      });
    });

    if (dom.changeBtn) {
      dom.changeBtn.addEventListener('click', onChangeTheme);
    }

    if (dom.enterBtn) {
      dom.enterBtn.addEventListener('click', onEnter);
    }
  }

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */
  /* 8. Boot                                                                */
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  function init() {
    cacheDom();

    document.body.classList.add('is-loading');
    document.body.classList.add('is-booting');

    bindEvents();
    initParallax();

    var saved = Theme.get();
    var isReturning = !!saved;

    runLoader().then(reveal).then(function () {
      if (isReturning) {
        /* Restore the previous choice without re-prompting the visitor. */
        selectTheme(saved, {
          silent: true,
          force: true,
          returning: true
        });
      }
    });
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
  } else {
    init();
  }
})(window, document);


=======================================================================
FILE: b.sh
=======================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# repo-to-txt.sh — dump repo text files into ./EcoFac.txt
set -euo pipefail

if (( BASH_VERSINFO[0] < 4 || (BASH_VERSINFO[0] == 4 && BASH_VERSINFO[1] < 4) )); then
    echo "error: need bash >= 4.4 (found $BASH_VERSION)" >&2; exit 1
fi

REPO_ROOT="${1:-.}"
[[ -d "$REPO_ROOT" ]] || { echo "error: not a directory: $REPO_ROOT" >&2; exit 1; }
cd "$REPO_ROOT"

OUTPUT="portfolio.txt"

# ---- 1. Enumerate -----------------------------------------------------------
# If we're in a git work-tree, git already knows what's "source":
#   --cached            tracked files
#   --others            untracked files…
#   --exclude-standard  …minus anything matched by .gitignore
# .eco.bak, .venv/, __pycache__/, *.pyc, *.egg-info/ are all in .gitignore
# already — git drops them for us. No list to maintain.
if git rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    mapfile -t CANDIDATES < <(git ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard | sort)
else
    # Fallback for non-git trees — keep the find chain as a safety net.
    mapfile -t CANDIDATES < <(
        find . -type f \
            -not -path './.git/*'                   \
            -not -path '*/.git/*'                   \
            -not -path '*/.eco.bak/*'               \
            -not -path '*/__pycache__/*'            \
            -not -path '*/.mypy_cache/*'            \
            -not -path '*/.pytest_cache/*'          \
            -not -path '*/.ruff_cache/*'            \
            -not -path '*/.tox/*'                   \
            -not -path '*/.eggs/*'                  \
            -not -path '*/.cache/*'                 \
            -not -path '*/.ipynb_checkpoints/*'     \
            -not -path '*/.vscode/*'                \
            -not -path '*/.idea/*'                  \
            -not -path '*/node_modules/*'           \
            -not -path '*/.venv/*'                  \
            -not -path '*/venv/*'                   \
            -not -path '*/env/*'                    \
            -not -path '*/virtualenv/*'             \
            -not -path '*/.virtualenv/*'            \
            -not -path '*/site-packages/*'          \
            -not -path '*/htmlcov/*'                \
            -not -path '*/*.egg-info/*'             \
            -not -path '*/*.dist-info/*'            \
            -not -path '*/.ecofac.bak/*'            \
            -not -path './build/*'                  \
	    -not -path '*.sh'
            -not -path './dist/*'                   \
            -not -name '*.pyc'   -not -name '*.pyo' -not -name '*.pyd' \
            -not -name '*.so'    -not -name '*.dylib' -not -name '*.dll' -not -name '*.exe' \
            -not -name '*.egg'   -not -name '.coverage' -not -name 'coverage.xml' \
            -not -name '*.db'    -not -name '*.sqlite' -not -name '*.sqlite3' \
            -not -name '*.log'   -not -name '.DS_Store' \
            -not -name '*.zip'   -not -name '*.tar'   -not -name '*.tgz'  \
            -not -name '*.gz'    -not -name '*.bz2'   -not -name '*.xz'   -not -name '*.7z' \
            -not -name '*.png'   -not -name '*.jpg'   -not -name '*.jpeg' \
            -not -name '*.gif'   -not -name '*.bmp'   -not -name '*.ico'  \
            -not -name '*.webp'  -not -name '*.svg'   -not -name '*.pdf'  \
            -not -name '*.woff'  -not -name '*.woff2' -not -name '*.ttf'  \
            -not -name '*.otf'   -not -name '*.eot'   \
            -not -name '*.mp3'   -not -name '*.mp4'   -not -name '*.mov'  -not -name '*.wav' \
            | sort
    )
fi

# ---- 2. Filter (self, meta, binary sniff) -----------------------------------
FILES=()
for f in "${CANDIDATES[@]}"; do
    # strip leading ./ that find emits, but git ls-files does not
    f="${f#./}"
    [[ -f "$f" ]] || continue
    # never include our own output or the tree dump
    [[ "$f" == "$OUTPUT"  ]] && continue
    [[ "$f" == "tree.txt" ]] && continue
    # binary sniff
    if [[ -s "$f" ]] && ! grep -Iq . -- "$f" 2>/dev/null; then
        continue
    fi
    FILES+=("$f")
done

# ---- 3. Header + index ------------------------------------------------------
{
    echo "# EcoFac — Whole Project Repository Snapshot"
    echo "# Generated : $(date -u +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")"
    echo "# Root      : $(pwd)"
    echo "# Files     : ${#FILES[@]}"
    echo ""
    echo "## File Index"
    echo ""
    for f in "${FILES[@]}"; do echo "  $f"; done
    echo ""
} > "$OUTPUT"

# ---- 4. Contents ------------------------------------------------------------
for f in "${FILES[@]}"; do
    {
        echo "======================================================================="
        echo "FILE: $f"
        echo "======================================================================="
        cat -- "$f"
        echo ""
        echo ""
    } >> "$OUTPUT"
done

printf 'Wrote %s — %d files, %s bytes\n' \
       "$OUTPUT" "${#FILES[@]}" "$(wc -c < "$OUTPUT")"

        <aside class="who__aside" data-reveal>
          <div class="who__portrait" aria-hidden="true"></div>
          <div class="who__caption">
            <span>Pakistan</span>
            <span>ICS</span>
          </div>
        </aside>

        <div class="who__body">
          <p data-reveal>
            I'm an <em>Intermediate in Computer Science (ICS)</em> student based in Pakistan.
            My pull toward technology has always been about understanding — how something works
            beneath the surface, why a system behaves the way it does, and what becomes possible
            once you actually understand it.
          </p>
          <p data-reveal>
            Most of what I know came from independent exploration rather than a syllabus:
            reading documentation, breaking things, rebuilding them, and following curiosity
            into unfamiliar territory. <em>AI, programming, cybersecurity and physics</em> each
            offer a different angle on the same underlying question — how do complex systems
            actually work?
          </p>
          <p data-reveal>
            I'd rather build a small, imperfect project that teaches me something real than
            read about one that doesn't. That's the pattern that has stuck: learn by building,
            understand by experimenting, and keep going.
          </p>

          <ul class="who__tags" data-reveal>
            <li class="who__tag">Independent learner</li>
            <li class="who__tag">Self-directed</li>
            <li class="who__tag">Building in public</li>
          </ul>
        </div>

             target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">
            GitHub
            <svg viewBox="0 0 14 14" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
              <path d="M3 11 11 3M5 3h6v6"/>

  <script src="../assets/js/site.js" defer></script>

  <script>
  /* ==========================================================================
     Contact page — page-specific behaviour
     Shared nav / theme / reveal / parallax behaviour is in site.js.
     This block adds:
       - footer year
       - copy email button
       - character counter
       - form validation + mailto submission
     ========================================================================== */
  (function () {
    'use strict';

    var reduced = document.documentElement.classList.contains('no-motion');

    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- Footer year */
    (function setYear() {
      var el = document.getElementById('footerYear');
      if (el) { el.textContent = String(new Date().getFullYear()); }
    })();

    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- Copy email */
    (function initCopy() {
      var btn = document.querySelector('.contact-card__btn--copy');
      if (!btn) { return; }
      var email = btn.getAttribute('data-copy') || '';
      if (!email) { return; }

      function flash(state) {
        btn.classList.add('is-copied');
        var originalHTML = btn.innerHTML;
        btn.innerHTML = state === 'done'
          ? 'Copied&nbsp;✓'
          : 'Copy failed';

        window.setTimeout(function () {
          btn.classList.remove('is-copied');
          btn.innerHTML = originalHTML;
        }, 1600);
      }

      btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
        if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
          navigator.clipboard.writeText(email).then(function () {
            flash('done');
          }).catch(function () {
            legacyCopy(email, flash);
          });
        } else {
          legacyCopy(email, flash);
        }
      });

      function legacyCopy(text, done) {
        var ta = document.createElement('textarea');
        ta.value = text;
        ta.setAttribute('readonly', '');
        ta.style.position = 'absolute';
        ta.style.left = '-9999px';
        document.body.appendChild(ta);
        ta.select();
        try {
          var ok = document.execCommand('copy');
          done(ok ? 'done' : 'fail');
        } catch (e) {
          done('fail');
        } finally {
          document.body.removeChild(ta);
        }
      }
    })();

    /* -------------------------------------------------------------- Form */
    (function initForm() {
      var form = document.getElementById('contactForm');
      if (!form) { return; }

      var status = document.getElementById('formStatus');
      var submitBtn = form.querySelector('.form-submit');
      var msgInput = form.querySelector('textarea[name="message"]');
      var charCount = document.getElementById('charCount');
      var email = 'amanravian1289@gmail.com';

      /* Take over HTML5 validation: our JS produces better, consistent UX.
         Without JS, the browser still validates and the mailto action works. */
      form.setAttribute('novalidate', 'true');

      /* ---------- character counter ---------- */
      function updateCount() {
        if (charCount && msgInput) {
          charCount.textContent = String(msgInput.value.length);
        }
      }
      if (msgInput) {
        updateCount();
        msgInput.addEventListener('input', updateCount);
      }

      /* ---------- validation ---------- */
      function isValidEmail(value) {
        /* Reasonably strict: local@domain.tld with at least 2 char TLD */
        return /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]{2,}$/.test(value);
      }

      function getError(field) {
        var value = (field.value || '').trim();
        var name = field.name;

        if (!value) {
          return getRequiredMsg(name);
        }
        if (name === 'email' && !isValidEmail(value)) {
          return 'Please enter a valid email address.';
        }
        if (name === 'name' && value.length < 2) {
          return 'Name must be at least 2 characters.';
        }
        if (name === 'subject' && value.length < 3) {
          return 'Subject must be at least 3 characters.';
        }
        if (name === 'message' && value.length < 20) {
          return 'Message must be at least 20 characters.';
        }
        if (name === 'message' && value.length > 2000) {
          return 'Message must be under 2000 characters.';
        }
        return '';
      }

      function getRequiredMsg(name) {
        switch (name) {
          case 'name':    return 'Please enter your name.';
          case 'email':   return 'Please enter your email address.';
          case 'subject': return 'Please enter a subject.';
          case 'message': return 'Please write a message.';
          default:        return 'This field is required.';
        }
      }

      function showError(field, message) {
        var row = field.closest('.form-row');
        var err = document.getElementById('err-' + field.name);
        if (err) { err.textContent = message; }
        if (row) { row.classList.add('has-error'); }
        field.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
        if (err) { field.setAttribute('aria-describedby', err.id); }
      }

      function clearError(field) {
        var row = field.closest('.form-row');
        var err = document.getElementById('err-' + field.name);
        if (err) { err.textContent = ''; }
        if (row) { row.classList.remove('has-error'); }
        field.removeAttribute('aria-invalid');
      }

      /* ---------- clear error as user types ---------- */
      var fields = Array.prototype.slice.call(
        form.querySelectorAll('input[name], textarea[name]')
      );
      fields.forEach(function (field) {
        field.addEventListener('input', function () {
          if (field.getAttribute('aria-invalid') === 'true') {
            var msg = getError(field);
            if (msg) {
              /* still invalid — refresh message */
              var err = document.getElementById('err-' + field.name);
              if (err) { err.textContent = msg; }
            } else {
              clearError(field);
            }
          }
        });
      });

      /* ---------- build mailto URL ---------- */
      function buildMailto(data) {
        var subject = 'Portfolio contact — ' + data.subject;
        var body = [
          'From: ' + data.name + ' <' + data.email + '>',
          '',
          data.message,
          '',
          '---',
          'Sent via the contact form on the portfolio site.'
        ].join('\n');

        return 'mailto:' + email
          + '?subject=' + encodeURIComponent(subject)
          + '&body=' + encodeURIComponent(body);
      }

      /* ---------- status rendering ---------- */
      function showStatus(state, title, bodyHTML) {
        if (!status) { return; }
        status.setAttribute('data-state', state);
        status.innerHTML =
          '<p class="form-status__title">' + title + '</p>'
          + '<div class="form-status__body">' + bodyHTML + '</div>';
        status.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduced ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'center' });
      }

      function clearStatus() {
        if (!status) { return; }
        status.removeAttribute('data-state');
        status.innerHTML = '';
      }

      /* ---------- submit ---------- */
      form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        clearStatus();

        var data = {
          name:    form.querySelector('input[name="name"]').value.trim(),
          email:   form.querySelector('input[name="email"]').value.trim(),
          subject: form.querySelector('input[name="subject"]').value.trim(),
          message: form.querySelector('textarea[name="message"]').value.trim()
        };

        /* Validate every field */
        var firstInvalid = null;
        fields.forEach(function (field) {
          var msg = getError(field);
          if (msg) {
            showError(field, msg);
            if (!firstInvalid) { firstInvalid = field; }
          } else {
            clearError(field);
          }
        });

        if (firstInvalid) {
          firstInvalid.focus({ preventScroll: false });
          showStatus(
            'error',
            'Please check the form',
            'Some fields need attention. Review the highlighted items above and try again.'
          );
          return;
        }

        /* Open the mailto — user's email app takes it from here */
        submitBtn.disabled = true;
        var mailtoUrl = buildMailto(data);

        /* Use location.href for best mailto handling across browsers.
           After a short delay, show success — email app should be opening. */
        try {
          window.location.href = mailtoUrl;
        } catch (e) {
          /* No email app configured, or browser blocked it */
        }

        window.setTimeout(function () {
          submitBtn.disabled = false;

          var fallbackHTML =
            'Your email application should open with the message prefilled. '
            + 'If nothing happened, you can '
            + '<a href="mailto:' + email + '" style="color:var(--accent);text-decoration:underline">open the email app manually</a> '
            + 'or copy the address below.';

          showStatus('success', 'Ready to send', fallbackHTML);

          /* Add a copy button inside the status block */
          if (status) {
            var actions = document.createElement('div');
            actions.className = 'form-status__actions';
            var copyBtn = document.createElement('button');
            copyBtn.type = 'button';
            copyBtn.className = 'contact-card__btn contact-card__btn--copy';
            copyBtn.setAttribute('data-copy', email);
            copyBtn.innerHTML = 'Copy email address';
            copyBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
              if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
                navigator.clipboard.writeText(email).then(function () {
                  copyBtn.textContent = 'Copied ✓';
                  window.setTimeout(function () {
                    copyBtn.textContent = 'Copy email address';
                  }, 1500);
                });
              } else {
                var ta = document.createElement('textarea');
                ta.value = email;
                ta.style.position = 'absolute';
                ta.style.left = '-9999px';
                document.body.appendChild(ta);
                ta.select();
                try { document.execCommand('copy'); } catch (e) {}
                document.body.removeChild(ta);
                copyBtn.textContent = 'Copied ✓';
                window.setTimeout(function () {
                  copyBtn.textContent = 'Copy email address';
                }, 1500);
              }
            });
            actions.appendChild(copyBtn);
            status.appendChild(actions);
          }
        }, 600);
      });
    })();
  })();
  </script>

</body>
</html>

    <!-- ═══════════════════════════ PHILOSOPHY ═══════════════════════════ -->
    <section class="section" id="philosophy" aria-labelledby="philTitle">
      <header class="section__head">
        <p class="section__label" data-reveal>05 — Philosophy</p>
        <h2 class="section__title" id="philTitle" data-reveal>Learning by building.</h2>
      </header>

      <div class="philosophy-lb" data-reveal>
        <p class="philosophy-lb__mark">Approach</p>
        <blockquote class="philosophy-lb__quote">
          I learn by breaking complex problems into smaller parts,
          <em>experimenting</em> with solutions, examining failures and
          <em>improving</em> what I build.
        </blockquote>

        <ul class="philosophy-lb__traits">
          <li class="philosophy-lb__trait">Independent learning</li>
          <li class="philosophy-lb__trait">Technical research</li>

  <script>
  /* ==========================================================================
     Experience page — page-specific behaviour
     Everything shared (nav, mobile menu, theme switcher, reveal, parallax) is
     handled by site.js. This block adds only the expand/collapse for the
     employment responsibilities panel.
     ========================================================================== */
  (function () {
    'use strict';

    var card = document.querySelector('.employment-card');
    if (!card) { return; }
    var toggle = card.querySelector('.employment-card__toggle');
    if (!toggle) { return; }

    toggle.addEventListener('click', function () {
      var open = card.classList.toggle('is-open');
      toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', open ? 'true' : 'false');

      /* Update button label (keeps screen-reader text in sync). */
      var labelNode = null;
      for (var i = 0; i < toggle.childNodes.length; i += 1) {
        var node = toggle.childNodes[i];
        if (node.nodeType === 3 && node.textContent.trim()) {
          labelNode = node;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (labelNode) {
        labelNode.textContent = open
          ? ' Hide responsibilities '
          : ' View responsibilities ';
      }
    });
  })();
  </script>

            <p class="project__desc">
              An open energy intelligence engine for facilities, designed to analyze
              electricity meter data, establish historical baselines and detect
              unusual energy consumption patterns.
            </p>

            <ul class="project__caps">
              <li>CSV meter-data ingestion</li>
              <li>Data-quality assessment</li>
              <li>Historical baseline computation</li>
              <li>Statistical anomaly detection</li>
              <li>Deterministic evidence attached to findings</li>
              <li>Incident lifecycle tracking</li>
              <li>Before / after energy verification</li>
              <li>Reproducible evaluation runner</li>
            </ul>

            <h3 class="project__sub">Technologies</h3>
            <ul class="project__tech">
              <li>Python 3.11</li>

              <li>REST APIs</li>
              <li>pytest</li>
              <li>GitHub Actions</li>
              <li>Render</li>
            </ul>

            <p class="project__note">
              <strong>Accuracy note —</strong> EcoFac currently uses synthetic data.
              Its methods are statistical and it is not yet production-ready.
              No verified energy savings are claimed.
            </p>

            <p class="project__category">Android Development · Music Applications</p>
            <h2 class="project__name" id="relyName">RELY</h2>
            <div class="project__status">

            </div>

        <div class="project__rely">

          <!-- Info column -->
          <div class="project__info project__info--left" data-reveal>
            <p class="project__desc">
              A lightweight, pixel-inspired Android music player designed around a
              distinctive monochrome aesthetic and personal Google Drive music
              storage.
            </p>

            <h3 class="project__sub">Design objectives</h3>
            <ul class="project__caps project__caps--design">
              <li>Pixel-inspired visual identity</li>
              <li>Monochrome interface</li>
              <li>Material 3 design language</li>
              <li>Lightweight performance</li>
              <li>Compatibility with low-end Android devices</li>
              <li>Streaming from the user's own Google Drive</li>
            </ul>

            <h3 class="project__sub">Technologies</h3>
            <ul class="project__tech">
              <li>Kotlin</li>
              <li>Android SDK</li>
              <li>Material 3</li>
              <li>Google Drive API</li>
            </ul>

            <p class="project__note">
              <strong>Status —</strong> RELY is currently in development.
              Features listed above are <em>design objectives</em>, not
              completed capabilities. No released app is claimed.
            </p>

}

}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   1. HERO — floating communication glyphs
   -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */

.hero__float {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 1;
  overflow: hidden;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.hero__float-item {
  position: absolute;
  left: var(--x);
  top: var(--y);
  width: var(--size, 22px);
  height: var(--size, 22px);
  color: var(--text-faint);
  opacity: 0.22;
  animation: floatDrift var(--dur, 12s) ease-in-out infinite;
  animation-delay: var(--delay, 0s);
}

.hero__float-item svg {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  display: block;
}

html[data-theme="cyberpunk"] .hero__float-item {
  color: var(--accent-3);
  opacity: 0.28;
  filter: drop-shadow(0 0 6px var(--accent-3));
}

html[data-theme="rich"] .hero__float-item {
  color: var(--accent);
  opacity: 0.32;
}

@keyframes floatDrift {
  0%, 100% { transform: translate3d(0, 0, 0) rotate(0deg); }
  25%      { transform: translate3d(8px, -14px, 0) rotate(6deg); }
  50%      { transform: translate3d(-4px, -22px, 0) rotate(-3deg); }
  75%      { transform: translate3d(-12px, -8px, 0) rotate(4deg); }
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .hero__float-item { animation: none; }
}
